\mbox{\Hypertarget{mac80211_8h_a0e0ce9245279242deb81ca6ae3f14905}\label{mac80211_8h_a0e0ce9245279242deb81ca6ae3f14905}} 
\index{mac80211.h@{mac80211.h}!mac80211\_tx\_info\_flags@{mac80211\_tx\_info\_flags}}
\index{mac80211\_tx\_info\_flags@{mac80211\_tx\_info\_flags}!mac80211.h@{mac80211.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{mac80211\_tx\_info\_flags}{mac80211\_tx\_info\_flags}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \mbox{\hyperlink{interfaceenum}{enum}} \mbox{\hyperlink{mac80211_8h_a0e0ce9245279242deb81ca6ae3f14905}{mac80211\+\_\+tx\+\_\+info\+\_\+flags}}}

enum mac80211\+\_\+tx\+\_\+info\+\_\+flags -\/ flags to describe transmission information/status

These flags are used with the @flags member of \&\mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__tx__info}{ieee80211\+\_\+tx\+\_\+info}}.

@\+I\+E\+E\+E80211\+\_\+\+T\+X\+\_\+\+C\+T\+L\+\_\+\+R\+E\+Q\+\_\+\+T\+X\+\_\+\+S\+T\+A\+T\+US\+: require TX status callback for this frame. @\+I\+E\+E\+E80211\+\_\+\+T\+X\+\_\+\+C\+T\+L\+\_\+\+A\+S\+S\+I\+G\+N\+\_\+\+S\+EQ\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} driver has to assign a sequence number to this frame, taking care of not overwriting the fragment number and increasing the sequence number only when the I\+E\+E\+E80211\+\_\+\+T\+X\+\_\+\+C\+T\+L\+\_\+\+F\+I\+R\+S\+T\+\_\+\+F\+R\+A\+G\+M\+E\+NT flag is set. mac80211 will properly assign sequence numbers to Qo\+S-\/data frames but cannot do so correctly for non-\/\+Qo\+S-\/data and management frames because beacons need them from that counter as well and mac80211 cannot guarantee proper sequencing. If this flag is set, the driver should instruct the hardware to assign a sequence number to the frame or assign one itself. Cf. I\+E\+EE 802.\+11-\/2007 7.\+1.\+3.\+4.\+1 paragraph 3. This flag will always be set for beacons and always be clear for frames without a sequence number field. @\+I\+E\+E\+E80211\+\_\+\+T\+X\+\_\+\+C\+T\+L\+\_\+\+N\+O\+\_\+\+A\+CK\+: tell the low level not to wait for an ack @\+I\+E\+E\+E80211\+\_\+\+T\+X\+\_\+\+C\+T\+L\+\_\+\+C\+L\+E\+A\+R\+\_\+\+P\+S\+\_\+\+F\+I\+LT\+: clear powersave filter for destination station @\+I\+E\+E\+E80211\+\_\+\+T\+X\+\_\+\+C\+T\+L\+\_\+\+F\+I\+R\+S\+T\+\_\+\+F\+R\+A\+G\+M\+E\+NT\+: this is a first fragment of the frame @\+I\+E\+E\+E80211\+\_\+\+T\+X\+\_\+\+C\+T\+L\+\_\+\+S\+E\+N\+D\+\_\+\+A\+F\+T\+E\+R\+\_\+\+D\+T\+IM\+: send this frame after D\+T\+IM beacon @\+I\+E\+E\+E80211\+\_\+\+T\+X\+\_\+\+C\+T\+L\+\_\+\+A\+M\+P\+DU\+: this frame should be sent as part of an A-\/\+M\+P\+DU @\+I\+E\+E\+E80211\+\_\+\+T\+X\+\_\+\+C\+T\+L\+\_\+\+I\+N\+J\+E\+C\+T\+ED\+: Frame was injected, internal to mac80211. @\+I\+E\+E\+E80211\+\_\+\+T\+X\+\_\+\+S\+T\+A\+T\+\_\+\+T\+X\+\_\+\+F\+I\+L\+T\+E\+R\+ED\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} frame was not transmitted because the destination S\+TA was in powersave mode. Note that to avoid race conditions, the filter must be set by the hardware or firmware upon receiving a frame that indicates that the station went to sleep (must be done on device to filter frames already on the queue) and may only be unset after mac80211 gives the OK for that by setting the I\+E\+E\+E80211\+\_\+\+T\+X\+\_\+\+C\+T\+L\+\_\+\+C\+L\+E\+A\+R\+\_\+\+P\+S\+\_\+\+F\+I\+LT (see above), since only then is it guaranteed that no more frames are in the hardware queue. @\+I\+E\+E\+E80211\+\_\+\+T\+X\+\_\+\+S\+T\+A\+T\+\_\+\+A\+CK\+: Frame was acknowledged @\+I\+E\+E\+E80211\+\_\+\+T\+X\+\_\+\+S\+T\+A\+T\+\_\+\+A\+M\+P\+DU\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} frame was aggregated, so status is for the whole aggregation. @\+I\+E\+E\+E80211\+\_\+\+T\+X\+\_\+\+S\+T\+A\+T\+\_\+\+A\+M\+P\+D\+U\+\_\+\+N\+O\+\_\+\+B\+A\+CK\+: no block ack was returned, so consider using block ack request (B\+AR). @\+I\+E\+E\+E80211\+\_\+\+T\+X\+\_\+\+C\+T\+L\+\_\+\+R\+A\+T\+E\+\_\+\+C\+T\+R\+L\+\_\+\+P\+R\+O\+BE\+: internal to mac80211, can be set by rate control algorithms to indicate probe rate, will be cleared for fragmented frames (except on the last fragment) @\+I\+E\+E\+E80211\+\_\+\+T\+X\+\_\+\+I\+N\+T\+F\+L\+\_\+\+O\+F\+F\+C\+H\+A\+N\+\_\+\+T\+X\+\_\+\+OK\+: Internal to mac80211. Used to indicate that a frame can be transmitted while the queues are stopped for off-\/channel operation. @\+I\+E\+E\+E80211\+\_\+\+T\+X\+\_\+\+C\+T\+L\+\_\+\+H\+W\+\_\+80211\+\_\+\+E\+N\+C\+AP\+: This frame uses hardware encapsulation (header conversion) @\+I\+E\+E\+E80211\+\_\+\+T\+X\+\_\+\+I\+N\+T\+F\+L\+\_\+\+R\+E\+T\+R\+I\+ED\+: completely internal to mac80211, used to indicate that a frame was already retried due to PS @\+I\+E\+E\+E80211\+\_\+\+T\+X\+\_\+\+I\+N\+T\+F\+L\+\_\+\+D\+O\+N\+T\+\_\+\+E\+N\+C\+R\+Y\+PT\+: completely internal to mac80211, used to indicate frame should not be encrypted @\+I\+E\+E\+E80211\+\_\+\+T\+X\+\_\+\+C\+T\+L\+\_\+\+N\+O\+\_\+\+P\+S\+\_\+\+B\+U\+F\+F\+ER\+: This frame is a response to a poll frame (P\+S-\/\+Poll or u\+A\+P\+SD) or a non-\/bufferable M\+M\+P\+DU and must be sent although the station is in powersave mode. @\+I\+E\+E\+E80211\+\_\+\+T\+X\+\_\+\+C\+T\+L\+\_\+\+M\+O\+R\+E\+\_\+\+F\+R\+A\+M\+ES\+: More frames will be passed to the transmit function after the current frame, this can be used by drivers to kick the D\+MA queue only if unset or when the queue gets full. @\+I\+E\+E\+E80211\+\_\+\+T\+X\+\_\+\+I\+N\+T\+F\+L\+\_\+\+R\+E\+T\+R\+A\+N\+S\+M\+I\+S\+S\+I\+ON\+: This frame is being retransmitted after TX status because the destination was asleep, it must not be modified again (no seqno assignment, crypto, etc.) @\+I\+E\+E\+E80211\+\_\+\+T\+X\+\_\+\+I\+N\+T\+F\+L\+\_\+\+M\+L\+M\+E\+\_\+\+C\+O\+N\+N\+\_\+\+TX\+: This frame was transmitted by the M\+L\+ME code for connection establishment, this indicates that its status should kick the M\+L\+ME state machine. @\+I\+E\+E\+E80211\+\_\+\+T\+X\+\_\+\+I\+N\+T\+F\+L\+\_\+\+N\+L80211\+\_\+\+F\+R\+A\+M\+E\+\_\+\+TX\+: Frame was requested through nl80211 M\+L\+ME command (internal to mac80211 to figure out whether to send TX status to user space) @\+I\+E\+E\+E80211\+\_\+\+T\+X\+\_\+\+C\+T\+L\+\_\+\+L\+D\+PC\+: tells the driver to use L\+D\+PC for this frame @\+I\+E\+E\+E80211\+\_\+\+T\+X\+\_\+\+C\+T\+L\+\_\+\+S\+T\+BC\+: Enables Space-\/\+Time Block Coding (S\+T\+BC) for this frame and selects the maximum number of streams that it can use. @\+I\+E\+E\+E80211\+\_\+\+T\+X\+\_\+\+C\+T\+L\+\_\+\+T\+X\+\_\+\+O\+F\+F\+C\+H\+AN\+: Marks this packet to be transmitted on the off-\/channel channel when a remain-\/on-\/channel offload is done in hardware -- normal packets still flow and are expected to be handled properly by the device. @\+I\+E\+E\+E80211\+\_\+\+T\+X\+\_\+\+I\+N\+T\+F\+L\+\_\+\+T\+K\+I\+P\+\_\+\+M\+I\+C\+\_\+\+F\+A\+I\+L\+U\+RE\+: Marks this packet to be used for T\+K\+IP testing. It will be sent out with incorrect Michael M\+IC key to allow T\+K\+IP countermeasures to be tested. @\+I\+E\+E\+E80211\+\_\+\+T\+X\+\_\+\+C\+T\+L\+\_\+\+N\+O\+\_\+\+C\+C\+K\+\_\+\+R\+A\+TE\+: This frame will be sent at non C\+CK rate. This flag is actually used for management frame especially for P2P frames not being sent at C\+CK rate in 2G\+Hz band. @\+I\+E\+E\+E80211\+\_\+\+T\+X\+\_\+\+S\+T\+A\+T\+U\+S\+\_\+\+E\+O\+SP\+: This packet marks the end of service period, when its status is reported the service period ends. For frames in an SP that mac80211 transmits, it is already set; for driver frames the driver may set this flag. It is also used to do the same for P\+S-\/\+Poll responses. @\+I\+E\+E\+E80211\+\_\+\+T\+X\+\_\+\+C\+T\+L\+\_\+\+U\+S\+E\+\_\+\+M\+I\+N\+R\+A\+TE\+: This frame will be sent at lowest rate. This flag is used to send nullfunc frame at minimum rate when the nullfunc is used for connection monitoring purpose. @\+I\+E\+E\+E80211\+\_\+\+T\+X\+\_\+\+C\+T\+L\+\_\+\+D\+O\+N\+T\+F\+R\+AG\+: Don\textquotesingle{}t fragment this packet even if it would be fragmented by size (this is optional, only used for monitor injection). @\+I\+E\+E\+E80211\+\_\+\+T\+X\+\_\+\+S\+T\+A\+T\+\_\+\+N\+O\+A\+C\+K\+\_\+\+T\+R\+A\+N\+S\+M\+I\+T\+T\+ED\+: A frame that was marked with I\+E\+E\+E80211\+\_\+\+T\+X\+\_\+\+C\+T\+L\+\_\+\+N\+O\+\_\+\+A\+CK has been successfully transmitted without any errors (like issues specific to the driver/\+HW). This flag must not be set for frames that don\textquotesingle{}t request no-\/ack behaviour with I\+E\+E\+E80211\+\_\+\+T\+X\+\_\+\+C\+T\+L\+\_\+\+N\+O\+\_\+\+A\+CK.

Note\+: If you have to add new flags to the enumeration, then don\textquotesingle{}t forget to update I\+E\+E\+E80211\+\_\+\+T\+X\+\_\+\+T\+E\+M\+P\+O\+R\+A\+R\+Y\+\_\+\+F\+L\+A\+GS when necessary. \begin{DoxyEnumFields}{Enumerator}

{\footnotesize\ttfamily static struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__hw}{ieee80211\+\_\+hw}}$\ast$ ieee80211\+\_\+alloc\+\_\+hw (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{include_2linux_2types_8h_aad0f8411dfc957b4069125f330192f4f}{size\+\_\+t}}}]{priv\+\_\+data\+\_\+len,  }\item[{const struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__ops}{ieee80211\+\_\+ops}} $\ast$}]{ops }\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [inline]}, {\ttfamily [static]}}

ieee80211\+\_\+alloc\+\_\+hw -\/ Allocate a new hardware device

This must be called once for each hardware device. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} returned pointer must be used to refer to this device when calling other functions. mac80211 allocates a private data area for the driver pointed to by @priv in \&struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__hw}{ieee80211\+\_\+hw}}, the size of this area is given as @priv\+\_\+data\+\_\+len.

@priv\+\_\+data\+\_\+len\+: length of private data @ops\+: callbacks for this device

Return\+: A pointer to the new hardware device, or N\+U\+LL on error. Here is the call graph for this function\+:
% FIG 1
\mbox{\Hypertarget{mac80211_8h_aa490a6b45199419f400e69c493ed4358}\label{mac80211_8h_aa490a6b45199419f400e69c493ed4358}} 
\index{mac80211.h@{mac80211.h}!ieee80211\_alloc\_hw\_nm@{ieee80211\_alloc\_hw\_nm}}
\index{ieee80211\_alloc\_hw\_nm@{ieee80211\_alloc\_hw\_nm}!mac80211.h@{mac80211.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{ieee80211\_alloc\_hw\_nm()}{ieee80211\_alloc\_hw\_nm()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__hw}{ieee80211\+\_\+hw}}$\ast$ ieee80211\+\_\+alloc\+\_\+hw\+\_\+nm (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{include_2linux_2types_8h_aad0f8411dfc957b4069125f330192f4f}{size\+\_\+t}}}]{priv\+\_\+data\+\_\+len,  }\item[{const struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__ops}{ieee80211\+\_\+ops}} $\ast$}]{ops,  }\item[{const char $\ast$}]{requested\+\_\+name }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

ieee80211\+\_\+alloc\+\_\+hw\+\_\+nm -\/ Allocate a new hardware device

This must be called once for each hardware device. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} returned pointer must be used to refer to this device when calling other functions. mac80211 allocates a private data area for the driver pointed to by @priv in \&struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__hw}{ieee80211\+\_\+hw}}, the size of this area is given as @priv\+\_\+data\+\_\+len.

@priv\+\_\+data\+\_\+len\+: length of private data @ops\+: callbacks for this device @requested\+\_\+name\+: Requested name for this device. N\+U\+LL is valid value, and means use the default naming (phyd)

Return\+: A pointer to the new hardware device, or N\+U\+LL on error. Here is the caller graph for this function\+:
% FIG 2
\mbox{\Hypertarget{mac80211_8h_aabf662e14888ca5cc420b0ab8bdf1d9e}\label{mac80211_8h_aabf662e14888ca5cc420b0ab8bdf1d9e}} 
\index{mac80211.h@{mac80211.h}!ieee80211\_ap\_probereq\_get@{ieee80211\_ap\_probereq\_get}}
\index{ieee80211\_ap\_probereq\_get@{ieee80211\_ap\_probereq\_get}!mac80211.h@{mac80211.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{ieee80211\_ap\_probereq\_get()}{ieee80211\_ap\_probereq\_get()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structsk__buff}{sk\+\_\+buff}}$\ast$ ieee80211\+\_\+ap\+\_\+probereq\+\_\+get (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__hw}{ieee80211\+\_\+hw}} $\ast$}]{hw,  }\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__vif}{ieee80211\+\_\+vif}} $\ast$}]{vif }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

ieee80211\+\_\+ap\+\_\+probereq\+\_\+get -\/ retrieve a Probe Request template @hw\+: pointer obtained from \mbox{\hyperlink{mac80211_8h_a6595c4d65fb5b0288de8888048d23bee}{ieee80211\+\_\+alloc\+\_\+hw()}}. @vif\+: \&struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__vif}{ieee80211\+\_\+vif}} pointer from the add\+\_\+interface callback.

Creates a Probe Request template which can, for example, be uploaded to hardware. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} template is filled with bssid, ssid and supported rate information. This function must only be called from within the .bss\+\_\+info\+\_\+changed callback function and only in managed mode. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} function is only useful when the interface is associated, otherwise it will return N\+U\+LL.

Return\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} Probe Request template. N\+U\+LL on error. \mbox{\Hypertarget{mac80211_8h_acda31237744b1eea4e032b2757045696}\label{mac80211_8h_acda31237744b1eea4e032b2757045696}}

\index{mac80211.h@{mac80211.h}!ieee80211\_beacon\_get\_template@{ieee80211\_beacon\_get\_template}}
\index{ieee80211\_beacon\_get\_template@{ieee80211\_beacon\_get\_template}!mac80211.h@{mac80211.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{ieee80211\_beacon\_get\_template()}{ieee80211\_beacon\_get\_template()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structsk__buff}{sk\+\_\+buff}}$\ast$ ieee80211\+\_\+beacon\+\_\+get\+\_\+template (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__hw}{ieee80211\+\_\+hw}} $\ast$}]{hw,  }\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__vif}{ieee80211\+\_\+vif}} $\ast$}]{vif,  }\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__mutable__offsets}{ieee80211\+\_\+mutable\+\_\+offsets}} $\ast$}]{offs }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

ieee80211\+\_\+beacon\+\_\+get\+\_\+template -\/ beacon template generation function @hw\+: pointer obtained from \mbox{\hyperlink{mac80211_8h_a6595c4d65fb5b0288de8888048d23bee}{ieee80211\+\_\+alloc\+\_\+hw()}}. @vif\+: \&struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__vif}{ieee80211\+\_\+vif}} pointer from the add\+\_\+interface callback. @offs\+: \&struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__mutable__offsets}{ieee80211\+\_\+mutable\+\_\+offsets}} pointer to struct that will receive the offsets that may be updated by the driver.

If the driver implements beaconing modes, it must use this function to obtain the beacon template.

This function should be used if the beacon frames are generated by the device, and then the driver must use the returned beacon as the template \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} driver or the device are responsible to update the D\+T\+IM and, when applicable, the C\+SA count.

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} driver is responsible for freeing the returned skb.

Return\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} beacon template. N\+U\+LL on error. \mbox{\Hypertarget{mac80211_8h_a47323d882810413b2cfd559459097a9b}\label{mac80211_8h_a47323d882810413b2cfd559459097a9b}} 
\index{mac80211.h@{mac80211.h}!ieee80211\_beacon\_get\_tim@{ieee80211\_beacon\_get\_tim}}
\index{ieee80211\_beacon\_get\_tim@{ieee80211\_beacon\_get\_tim}!mac80211.h@{mac80211.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{ieee80211\_beacon\_get\_tim()}{ieee80211\_beacon\_get\_tim()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structsk__buff}{sk\+\_\+buff}}$\ast$ ieee80211\+\_\+beacon\+\_\+get\+\_\+tim (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__hw}{ieee80211\+\_\+hw}} $\ast$}]{hw,  }\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__vif}{ieee80211\+\_\+vif}} $\ast$}]{vif,  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{build_8c_a9e6c91d77e24643b888dbd1a1a590054}{u16}} $\ast$}]{tim\+\_\+offset,  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{build_8c_a9e6c91d77e24643b888dbd1a1a590054}{u16}} $\ast$}]{tim\+\_\+length }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

ieee80211\+\_\+beacon\+\_\+get\+\_\+tim -\/ beacon generation function @hw\+: pointer obtained from \mbox{\hyperlink{mac80211_8h_a6595c4d65fb5b0288de8888048d23bee}{ieee80211\+\_\+alloc\+\_\+hw()}}. @vif\+: \&struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__vif}{ieee80211\+\_\+vif}} pointer from the add\+\_\+interface callback. @tim\+\_\+offset\+: pointer to variable that will receive the T\+IM IE offset. Set to 0 if invalid (in non-\/\+AP modes). @tim\+\_\+length\+: pointer to variable that will receive the T\+IM IE length, (including the ID and length bytes!). Set to 0 if invalid (in non-\/\+AP modes).

If the driver implements beaconing modes, it must use this function to obtain the beacon frame.

If the beacon frames are generated by the host system (i.\+e., not in hardware/firmware), the driver uses this function to get each beacon frame from mac80211 -- it is responsible for calling this function exactly once before the beacon is needed (e.\+g. based on hardware interrupt).

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} driver is responsible for freeing the returned skb.

Return\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} beacon template. N\+U\+LL on error. Here is the caller graph for this function\+:
% FIG 4
\mbox{\Hypertarget{mac80211_8h_a24e3f5421aaa83511f6ab96d1e688b11}\label{mac80211_8h_a24e3f5421aaa83511f6ab96d1e688b11}}

When beacon filtering is enabled with I\+E\+E\+E80211\+\_\+\+V\+I\+F\+\_\+\+B\+E\+A\+C\+O\+N\+\_\+\+F\+I\+L\+T\+ER and I\+E\+E\+E80211\+\_\+\+C\+O\+N\+F\+\_\+\+PS is set, the driver needs to inform whenever the hardware is not receiving beacons with this function. \mbox{\Hypertarget{mac80211_8h_aa3a0e0026bbc2661585e5ca26d1003a1}\label{mac80211_8h_aa3a0e0026bbc2661585e5ca26d1003a1}}

ieee80211\+\_\+beacon\+\_\+set\+\_\+cntdwn -\/ request mac80211 to set beacon countdown @vif\+: \&struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__vif}{ieee80211\+\_\+vif}} pointer from the add\+\_\+interface callback. @counter\+: the new value for the counter

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} beacon countdown can be changed by the device, this A\+PI should be used by the device driver to update csa counter in mac80211.

It should never be used together with \mbox{\hyperlink{mac80211_8h_aeffe22301a8a45c0b5aab6d1fb0dd96c}{ieee80211\+\_\+beacon\+\_\+update\+\_\+cntdwn()}}, as it will cause a race condition around the counter value. \mbox{\Hypertarget{mac80211_8h_aeffe22301a8a45c0b5aab6d1fb0dd96c}\label{mac80211_8h_aeffe22301a8a45c0b5aab6d1fb0dd96c}}

\index{ieee80211\_beacon\_update\_cntdwn@{ieee80211\_beacon\_update\_cntdwn}!mac80211.h@{mac80211.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{ieee80211\_beacon\_update\_cntdwn()}{ieee80211\_beacon\_update\_cntdwn()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \mbox{\hyperlink{build_8c_aed742c436da53c1080638ce6ef7d13de}{u8}} ieee80211\+\_\+beacon\+\_\+update\+\_\+cntdwn (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__vif}{ieee80211\+\_\+vif}} $\ast$}]{vif }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

ieee80211\+\_\+beacon\+\_\+update\+\_\+cntdwn -\/ request mac80211 to decrement the beacon countdown @vif\+: \&struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__vif}{ieee80211\+\_\+vif}} pointer from the add\+\_\+interface callback.

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} beacon counter should be updated after each beacon transmission. This function is called implicitly when ieee80211\+\_\+beacon\+\_\+get/ieee80211\+\_\+beacon\+\_\+get\+\_\+tim are called, however if the beacon frames are generated by the device, the driver should call this function after each beacon transmission to sync mac80211\textquotesingle{}s beacon countdown.

Return\+: new countdown value \mbox{\Hypertarget{mac80211_8h_a5e5e4a766017c33c44499e58debfd3a9}\label{mac80211_8h_a5e5e4a766017c33c44499e58debfd3a9}}

{\footnotesize\ttfamily void ieee80211\+\_\+connection\+\_\+loss (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__vif}{ieee80211\+\_\+vif}} $\ast$}]{vif }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

ieee80211\+\_\+connection\+\_\+loss -\/ inform hardware has lost connection to the AP

@vif\+: \&struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__vif}{ieee80211\+\_\+vif}} pointer from the add\+\_\+interface callback.

When beacon filtering is enabled with I\+E\+E\+E80211\+\_\+\+V\+I\+F\+\_\+\+B\+E\+A\+C\+O\+N\+\_\+\+F\+I\+L\+T\+ER, and I\+E\+E\+E80211\+\_\+\+C\+O\+N\+F\+\_\+\+PS and I\+E\+E\+E80211\+\_\+\+H\+W\+\_\+\+C\+O\+N\+N\+E\+C\+T\+I\+O\+N\+\_\+\+M\+O\+N\+I\+T\+OR are set, the driver needs to inform if the connection to the AP has been lost. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} function may also be called if the connection needs to be terminated for some other reason, even if I\+E\+E\+E80211\+\_\+\+H\+W\+\_\+\+C\+O\+N\+N\+E\+C\+T\+I\+O\+N\+\_\+\+M\+O\+N\+I\+T\+OR isn\textquotesingle{}t set.

This function will cause immediate change to disassociated state, without connection recovery attempts. \mbox{\Hypertarget{mac80211_8h_ae21b128f021ecd1dbd7852453953e6b4}\label{mac80211_8h_ae21b128f021ecd1dbd7852453953e6b4}}

ieee80211\+\_\+cqm\+\_\+rssi\+\_\+notify -\/ inform a configured connection quality monitoring rssi threshold triggered

ieee80211\+\_\+ctstoself\+\_\+duration -\/ Get the duration field for a C\+T\+S-\/to-\/self frame @hw\+: pointer obtained from \mbox{\hyperlink{mac80211_8h_a6595c4d65fb5b0288de8888048d23bee}{ieee80211\+\_\+alloc\+\_\+hw()}}. @vif\+: \&struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__vif}{ieee80211\+\_\+vif}} pointer from the add\+\_\+interface callback. @frame\+\_\+len\+: the length of the frame that is going to be protected by the C\+T\+S-\/to-\/self. @frame\+\_\+txctl\+: \&struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__tx__info}{ieee80211\+\_\+tx\+\_\+info}} of the frame.

ieee80211\+\_\+ctstoself\+\_\+get -\/ C\+T\+S-\/to-\/self frame generation function @hw\+: pointer obtained from \mbox{\hyperlink{mac80211_8h_a6595c4d65fb5b0288de8888048d23bee}{ieee80211\+\_\+alloc\+\_\+hw()}}. @vif\+: \&struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__vif}{ieee80211\+\_\+vif}} pointer from the add\+\_\+interface callback. @frame\+: pointer to the frame that is going to be protected by the C\+T\+S-\/to-\/self. @frame\+\_\+len\+: the frame length (in octets). @frame\+\_\+txctl\+: \&struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__tx__info}{ieee80211\+\_\+tx\+\_\+info}} of the frame. @cts\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} buffer where to store the C\+T\+S-\/to-\/self frame.

ieee80211\+\_\+find\+\_\+sta -\/ find a station

@vif\+: virtual interface to look for station on @addr\+: station\textquotesingle{}s address

Return\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} station, if found. N\+U\+LL otherwise.

Note\+: This function must be called under R\+CU lock and the resulting pointer is only valid under R\+CU lock as well. \mbox{\Hypertarget{mac80211_8h_a096ee0554086d479ad7e62008d2b71b2}\label{mac80211_8h_a096ee0554086d479ad7e62008d2b71b2}} 
\index{mac80211.h@{mac80211.h}!ieee80211\_find\_sta\_by\_ifaddr@{ieee80211\_find\_sta\_by\_ifaddr}}
\index{ieee80211\_find\_sta\_by\_ifaddr@{ieee80211\_find\_sta\_by\_ifaddr}!mac80211.h@{mac80211.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{ieee80211\_find\_sta\_by\_ifaddr()}{ieee80211\_find\_sta\_by\_ifaddr()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__sta}{ieee80211\+\_\+sta}}$\ast$ ieee80211\+\_\+find\+\_\+sta\+\_\+by\+\_\+ifaddr (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__hw}{ieee80211\+\_\+hw}} $\ast$}]{hw,  }\item[{const \mbox{\hyperlink{build_8c_aed742c436da53c1080638ce6ef7d13de}{u8}} $\ast$}]{addr,  }\item[{const \mbox{\hyperlink{build_8c_aed742c436da53c1080638ce6ef7d13de}{u8}} $\ast$}]{localaddr }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

ieee80211\+\_\+find\+\_\+sta\+\_\+by\+\_\+ifaddr -\/ find a station on hardware

@hw\+: pointer as obtained from \mbox{\hyperlink{mac80211_8h_a6595c4d65fb5b0288de8888048d23bee}{ieee80211\+\_\+alloc\+\_\+hw()}} @addr\+: remote station\textquotesingle{}s address @localaddr\+: local address (vif-\/$>$sdata-\/$>$vif.\+addr). Use N\+U\+LL for \textquotesingle{}any\textquotesingle{}.

Return\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} station, if found. N\+U\+LL otherwise.

Note\+: This function must be called under R\+CU lock and the resulting pointer is only valid under R\+CU lock as well.

N\+O\+TE\+: You may pass N\+U\+LL for localaddr, but then you will just get the first S\+TA that matches the remote address \textquotesingle{}addr\textquotesingle{}. We can have multiple S\+TA associated with multiple logical stations (e.\+g. consider a station connecting to another B\+S\+S\+ID on the same AP hardware without disconnecting first). In this case, the result of this method with localaddr N\+U\+LL is not reliable.

DO N\+OT U\+SE T\+H\+IS F\+U\+N\+C\+T\+I\+ON with localaddr N\+U\+LL if at all possible. \mbox{\Hypertarget{mac80211_8h_aed151a159c200a1398b6557ae9e9e788}\label{mac80211_8h_aed151a159c200a1398b6557ae9e9e788}}

ieee80211\+\_\+generic\+\_\+frame\+\_\+duration -\/ Calculate the duration field for a frame @hw\+: pointer obtained from \mbox{\hyperlink{mac80211_8h_a6595c4d65fb5b0288de8888048d23bee}{ieee80211\+\_\+alloc\+\_\+hw()}}. @vif\+: \&struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__vif}{ieee80211\+\_\+vif}} pointer from the add\+\_\+interface callback. @band\+: the band to calculate the frame duration on @frame\+\_\+len\+: the length of the frame. @rate\+: the rate at which the frame is going to be transmitted.

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{ieee80211\_get\_assoc\_led\_name()}{ieee80211\_get\_assoc\_led\_name()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily static const char$\ast$ ieee80211\+\_\+get\+\_\+assoc\+\_\+led\+\_\+name (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__hw}{ieee80211\+\_\+hw}} $\ast$}]{hw }\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [inline]}, {\ttfamily [static]}}

ieee80211\+\_\+get\+\_\+assoc\+\_\+led\+\_\+name -\/ get name of association L\+ED

mac80211 creates a association L\+ED trigger for each wireless hardware that can be used to drive L\+E\+Ds if your driver registers a L\+ED device. This function returns the name (or N\+U\+LL if not configured for L\+E\+Ds) of the trigger so you can automatically link the L\+ED device.

@hw\+: the hardware to get the L\+ED trigger name for

Return\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} name of the L\+ED trigger. N\+U\+LL if not configured for L\+E\+Ds. \mbox{\Hypertarget{mac80211_8h_ade364bd5dffc9a60efde808b453bed16}\label{mac80211_8h_ade364bd5dffc9a60efde808b453bed16}} 
\index{mac80211.h@{mac80211.h}!ieee80211\_get\_buffered\_bc@{ieee80211\_get\_buffered\_bc}}
\index{ieee80211\_get\_buffered\_bc@{ieee80211\_get\_buffered\_bc}!mac80211.h@{mac80211.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{ieee80211\_get\_buffered\_bc()}{ieee80211\_get\_buffered\_bc()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structsk__buff}{sk\+\_\+buff}}$\ast$ ieee80211\+\_\+get\+\_\+buffered\+\_\+bc (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__hw}{ieee80211\+\_\+hw}} $\ast$}]{hw,  }\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__vif}{ieee80211\+\_\+vif}} $\ast$}]{vif }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

ieee80211\+\_\+get\+\_\+buffered\+\_\+bc -\/ accessing buffered broadcast and multicast frames @hw\+: pointer as obtained from \mbox{\hyperlink{mac80211_8h_a6595c4d65fb5b0288de8888048d23bee}{ieee80211\+\_\+alloc\+\_\+hw()}}. @vif\+: \&struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__vif}{ieee80211\+\_\+vif}} pointer from the add\+\_\+interface callback.

Function for accessing buffered broadcast and multicast frames. If hardware/firmware does not implement buffering of broadcast/multicast frames when power saving is used, 802.\+11 code buffers them in the host memory. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} low-\/level driver uses this function to fetch next buffered frame. In most cases, this is used when generating beacon frame.

Return\+: A pointer to the next buffered skb or N\+U\+LL if no more buffered frames are available.

Note\+: buffered frames are returned only after D\+T\+IM beacon frame was generated with \mbox{\hyperlink{mac80211_8h_a28a452f4a408454c40de46b03e704496}{ieee80211\+\_\+beacon\+\_\+get()}} and the low-\/level driver must thus call \mbox{\hyperlink{mac80211_8h_a28a452f4a408454c40de46b03e704496}{ieee80211\+\_\+beacon\+\_\+get()}} first. \mbox{\hyperlink{mac80211_8h_ade364bd5dffc9a60efde808b453bed16}{ieee80211\+\_\+get\+\_\+buffered\+\_\+bc()}} returns N\+U\+LL if the previous generated beacon was not D\+T\+IM, so the low-\/level driver does not need to check for D\+T\+IM beacons separately and should be able to use common code for all beacons. \mbox{\Hypertarget{mac80211_8h_a89023765df137e14efb98f20e2da32e6}\label{mac80211_8h_a89023765df137e14efb98f20e2da32e6}}

ieee80211\+\_\+get\+\_\+key\+\_\+rx\+\_\+seq -\/ get key RX sequence counter

@keyconf\+: the parameter passed with the set key @tid\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} T\+ID, or -\/1 for the management frame value (C\+C\+M\+P/\+G\+C\+MP only); the value on T\+ID 0 is also used for non-\/\+QoS frames. For C\+M\+AC, only T\+ID 0 is valid. @seq\+: buffer to receive the sequence data

This function allows a driver to retrieve the current RX I\+V/\+P\+Ns for the given key. It must not be called if IV checking is done by the device and not by mac80211.

Note that this function may only be called when no RX processing can be done concurrently. \mbox{\Hypertarget{mac80211_8h_a0637a0a621364d636bd50325cf4b4dfa}\label{mac80211_8h_a0637a0a621364d636bd50325cf4b4dfa}}

{\footnotesize\ttfamily struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__key__conf}{ieee80211\+\_\+key\+\_\+conf}}$\ast$ ieee80211\+\_\+gtk\+\_\+rekey\+\_\+add (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__vif}{ieee80211\+\_\+vif}} $\ast$}]{vif,  }\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__key__conf}{ieee80211\+\_\+key\+\_\+conf}} $\ast$}]{keyconf }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

ieee80211\+\_\+gtk\+\_\+rekey\+\_\+add -\/ add a G\+TK key from rekeying during Wo\+W\+L\+AN @vif\+: the virtual interface to add the key on @keyconf\+: new key data

When G\+TK rekeying was done while the system was suspended, (a) new key(s) will be available. These will be needed by mac80211 for proper RX processing, so this function allows setting them.

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} function returns the newly allocated key structure, which will have similar contents to the passed key configuration but point to mac80211-\/owned memory. In case of errors, the function returns an \mbox{\hyperlink{err_8h_ae49164ef6cf8117ec9f2fa9a8bb280ea}{E\+R\+R\+\_\+\+P\+T\+R()}}, use \mbox{\hyperlink{err_8h_a14e450a2ecb893a8c244a335097ba792}{I\+S\+\_\+\+E\+R\+R()}} etc.

Note that this function assumes the key isn\textquotesingle{}t added to hardware acceleration, so no TX will be done with the key. Since it\textquotesingle{}s a G\+TK on managed (station) networks, this is true anyway. If the driver calls this function from the resume callback and subsequently uses the return code 1 to reconfigure the device, this key will be part of the reconfiguration.

Note that the driver should also call \mbox{\hyperlink{mac80211_8h_a4547acecb32c5e39739f44a5ea933f6c}{ieee80211\+\_\+set\+\_\+key\+\_\+rx\+\_\+seq()}} for the new key for each T\+ID to set up sequence counters properly.

I\+M\+P\+O\+R\+T\+A\+NT\+: If this replaces a key that is present in the hardware, then it will attempt to remove it during this call. In many cases this isn\textquotesingle{}t what you want, so call \mbox{\hyperlink{mac80211_8h_a3be66d0ca1b9cd1d2660b86252871e1f}{ieee80211\+\_\+remove\+\_\+key()}} first for the key that\textquotesingle{}s being replaced. \mbox{\Hypertarget{mac80211_8h_ac61df7b6bce1d7fdee672d8d0e4a7a4f}\label{mac80211_8h_ac61df7b6bce1d7fdee672d8d0e4a7a4f}}

{\footnotesize\ttfamily void ieee80211\+\_\+iter\+\_\+chan\+\_\+contexts\+\_\+atomic (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__hw}{ieee80211\+\_\+hw}} $\ast$}]{hw,  }\item[{void($\ast$)(struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__hw}{ieee80211\+\_\+hw}} $\ast$hw, struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__chanctx__conf}{ieee80211\+\_\+chanctx\+\_\+conf}} $\ast$chanctx\+\_\+conf, void $\ast$\mbox{\hyperlink{pldmfw__private_8h_a6dc95797421616e7833cb107fee9d17f}{data}})}]{iter,  }\item[{void $\ast$}]{iter\+\_\+data }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

ieee80211\+\_\+iter\+\_\+chan\+\_\+contexts\+\_\+atomic -\/ iterate channel contexts @hw\+: pointer obtained from \mbox{\hyperlink{mac80211_8h_a6595c4d65fb5b0288de8888048d23bee}{ieee80211\+\_\+alloc\+\_\+hw()}}. @iter\+: iterator function @iter\+\_\+data\+: data passed to iterator function

Iterate all active channel contexts. This function is atomic and doesn\textquotesingle{}t acquire any locks internally that might be held in other places while calling into the driver.

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} iterator will not find a context that\textquotesingle{}s being added (during the driver callback to add it) but will find it while it\textquotesingle{}s being removed.

Note that during hardware restart, all contexts that existed before the restart are considered already present so will be found while iterating, whether they\textquotesingle{}ve been re-\/added already or not. \mbox{\Hypertarget{mac80211_8h_a8071260f151435a3aae6d93b1830f002}\label{mac80211_8h_a8071260f151435a3aae6d93b1830f002}}

{\footnotesize\ttfamily void ieee80211\+\_\+iter\+\_\+keys (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__hw}{ieee80211\+\_\+hw}} $\ast$}]{hw,  }\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__vif}{ieee80211\+\_\+vif}} $\ast$}]{vif,  }\item[{void($\ast$)(struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__hw}{ieee80211\+\_\+hw}} $\ast$hw, struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__vif}{ieee80211\+\_\+vif}} $\ast$vif, struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__sta}{ieee80211\+\_\+sta}} $\ast$sta, struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__key__conf}{ieee80211\+\_\+key\+\_\+conf}} $\ast$\mbox{\hyperlink{compress__offload_8h_a22ce381e6b944e029f74fa6c8d1ce549}{key}}, void $\ast$\mbox{\hyperlink{pldmfw__private_8h_a6dc95797421616e7833cb107fee9d17f}{data}})}]{iter,  }\item[{void $\ast$}]{iter\+\_\+data }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

ieee80211\+\_\+iter\+\_\+keys -\/ iterate keys programmed into the device @hw\+: pointer obtained from \mbox{\hyperlink{mac80211_8h_a6595c4d65fb5b0288de8888048d23bee}{ieee80211\+\_\+alloc\+\_\+hw()}} @vif\+: virtual interface to iterate, may be N\+U\+LL for all @iter\+: iterator function that will be called for each key @iter\+\_\+data\+: custom data to pass to the iterator function

This function can be used to iterate all the keys known to mac80211, even those that weren\textquotesingle{}t previously programmed into the device. This is intended for use in Wo\+W\+L\+AN if the device needs reprogramming of the keys during suspend. Note that due to locking reasons, it is also only safe to call this at few spots since it must hold the R\+T\+NL and be able to sleep.

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} order in which the keys are iterated matches the order in which they were originally installed and handed to the set\+\_\+key callback. \mbox{\Hypertarget{mac80211_8h_a16a8af76964247a8920a76fdbeea0c3a}\label{mac80211_8h_a16a8af76964247a8920a76fdbeea0c3a}}

{\footnotesize\ttfamily void ieee80211\+\_\+iter\+\_\+keys\+\_\+rcu (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__hw}{ieee80211\+\_\+hw}} $\ast$}]{hw,  }\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__vif}{ieee80211\+\_\+vif}} $\ast$}]{vif,  }\item[{void($\ast$)(struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__hw}{ieee80211\+\_\+hw}} $\ast$hw, struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__vif}{ieee80211\+\_\+vif}} $\ast$vif, struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__sta}{ieee80211\+\_\+sta}} $\ast$sta, struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__key__conf}{ieee80211\+\_\+key\+\_\+conf}} $\ast$\mbox{\hyperlink{compress__offload_8h_a22ce381e6b944e029f74fa6c8d1ce549}{key}}, void $\ast$\mbox{\hyperlink{pldmfw__private_8h_a6dc95797421616e7833cb107fee9d17f}{data}})}]{iter,  }\item[{void $\ast$}]{iter\+\_\+data }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

ieee80211\+\_\+iter\+\_\+keys\+\_\+rcu -\/ iterate keys programmed into the device @hw\+: pointer obtained from \mbox{\hyperlink{mac80211_8h_a6595c4d65fb5b0288de8888048d23bee}{ieee80211\+\_\+alloc\+\_\+hw()}} @vif\+: virtual interface to iterate, may be N\+U\+LL for all @iter\+: iterator function that will be called for each key @iter\+\_\+data\+: custom data to pass to the iterator function

This function can be used to iterate all the keys known to mac80211, even those that weren\textquotesingle{}t previously programmed into the device. Note that due to locking reasons, keys of station in removal process will be skipped.

This function requires being called in an R\+CU critical section, and thus iter must be atomic. \mbox{\Hypertarget{mac80211_8h_ac763147ffaf376215a3fb998eb2bb585}\label{mac80211_8h_ac763147ffaf376215a3fb998eb2bb585}}

ieee80211\+\_\+mark\+\_\+rx\+\_\+ba\+\_\+filtered\+\_\+frames -\/ move RX BA window and mark filtered @pubsta\+: station struct @tid\+: the session\textquotesingle{}s T\+ID @ssn\+: starting sequence number of the bitmap, all frames before this are assumed to be out of the window after the call @filtered\+: bitmap of filtered frames, \mbox{\hyperlink{vdso_2bits_8h_a4eaf4bf81c52c3f375967906e4d5ba17}{B\+I\+T(0)}} is the @ssn entry etc. @received\+\_\+mpdus\+: number of received mpdus in firmware

This function moves the BA window and releases all frames before @ssn, and marks frames marked in the bitmap as having been filtered. Afterwards, it checks if any frames in the window starting from @ssn can now be released (in case they were only waiting for frames that were filtered.) \mbox{\Hypertarget{mac80211_8h_ab2bbbdac255b54ab5d081f348ca63dd8}\label{mac80211_8h_ab2bbbdac255b54ab5d081f348ca63dd8}}

ieee80211\+\_\+nan\+\_\+func\+\_\+match -\/ notify about N\+AN function match event.

This function is used to notify mac80211 about N\+AN function match. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} cookie inside the match struct will be assigned by mac80211. Note that this function can\textquotesingle{}t be called from hard irq.

ieee80211\+\_\+nan\+\_\+func\+\_\+terminated -\/ notify about N\+AN function termination.

This function is used to notify mac80211 about N\+AN function termination. Note that this function can\textquotesingle{}t be called from hard irq.

@vif\+: \&struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__vif}{ieee80211\+\_\+vif}} pointer from the add\+\_\+interface callback. @inst\+\_\+id\+: the local instance id @reason\+: termination reason (one of the N\+L80211\+\_\+\+N\+A\+N\+\_\+\+F\+U\+N\+C\+\_\+\+T\+E\+R\+M\+\_\+\+R\+E\+A\+S\+O\+N\+\_\+$\ast$) @gfp\+: allocation flags \mbox{\Hypertarget{mac80211_8h_a2e02475b8a6ada611c4dc8d4f2d3edf2}\label{mac80211_8h_a2e02475b8a6ada611c4dc8d4f2d3edf2}}

ieee80211\+\_\+next\+\_\+txq -\/ get next tx queue to pull packets from

@hw\+: pointer as obtained from \mbox{\hyperlink{mac80211_8h_a6595c4d65fb5b0288de8888048d23bee}{ieee80211\+\_\+alloc\+\_\+hw()}} @ac\+: AC number to return packets from.

Returns the next txq if successful, N\+U\+LL if no queue is eligible. If a txq is returned, it should be returned with \mbox{\hyperlink{mac80211_8h_a807cba367501cc2ba87559872180865e}{ieee80211\+\_\+return\+\_\+txq()}} after the driver has finished scheduling it. \mbox{\Hypertarget{mac80211_8h_a8cff2be2ddd7c376da330f5f46e16d1c}\label{mac80211_8h_a8cff2be2ddd7c376da330f5f46e16d1c}}

ieee80211\+\_\+nullfunc\+\_\+get -\/ retrieve a nullfunc template @hw\+: pointer obtained from \mbox{\hyperlink{mac80211_8h_a6595c4d65fb5b0288de8888048d23bee}{ieee80211\+\_\+alloc\+\_\+hw()}}. @vif\+: \&struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__vif}{ieee80211\+\_\+vif}} pointer from the add\+\_\+interface callback. @qos\+\_\+ok\+: QoS N\+DP is acceptable to the caller, this should be set if at all possible

Creates a Nullfunc template which can, for example, uploaded to hardware. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} template must be updated after association so that correct B\+S\+S\+ID and address is used.

If @qos\+\_\+ndp is set and the association is to an AP with Qo\+S/\+W\+MM, the returned packet will be QoS N\+DP.

Note\+: Caller (or hardware) is responsible for setting the \&I\+E\+E\+E80211\+\_\+\+F\+C\+T\+L\+\_\+\+PM bit as well as Duration and Sequence Control fields.

Return\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} nullfunc template. N\+U\+LL on error. \mbox{\Hypertarget{mac80211_8h_ac9600ee7afe226409b06f4c68d2b3616}\label{mac80211_8h_ac9600ee7afe226409b06f4c68d2b3616}}

ieee80211\+\_\+parse\+\_\+tx\+\_\+radiotap -\/ Sanity-\/check and parse the radiotap header of injected frames.

To accurately parse and take into account rate and retransmission fields, you must initialize the chandef field in the \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__tx__info}{ieee80211\+\_\+tx\+\_\+info}} structure of the skb before calling this function.

ieee80211\+\_\+register\+\_\+hw -\/ Register hardware device

You must call this function before any other functions in mac80211. Note that before a hardware can be registered, you need to fill the contained wiphy\textquotesingle{}s information.

@hw\+: the device to register as returned by \mbox{\hyperlink{mac80211_8h_a6595c4d65fb5b0288de8888048d23bee}{ieee80211\+\_\+alloc\+\_\+hw()}}

Return\+: 0 on success. An error code otherwise. \mbox{\Hypertarget{mac80211_8h_a88846f8ed0c18e32f9c5f929dfe3b92b}\label{mac80211_8h_a88846f8ed0c18e32f9c5f929dfe3b92b}}

ieee80211\+\_\+remove\+\_\+key -\/ remove the given key @keyconf\+: the parameter passed with the set key

Remove the given key. If the key was uploaded to the hardware at the time this function is called, it is not deleted in the hardware but instead assumed to have been removed already.

Note that due to locking considerations this function can (currently) only be called during key iteration (\mbox{\hyperlink{mac80211_8h_a8071260f151435a3aae6d93b1830f002}{ieee80211\+\_\+iter\+\_\+keys()}}.) \mbox{\Hypertarget{mac80211_8h_a20eecd5bb1804bb74bd23f8f2d9cb93c}\label{mac80211_8h_a20eecd5bb1804bb74bd23f8f2d9cb93c}}

ieee80211\+\_\+request\+\_\+smps -\/ request SM PS transition @vif\+: \&struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__vif}{ieee80211\+\_\+vif}} pointer from the add\+\_\+interface callback. @smps\+\_\+mode\+: new SM PS mode

This allows the driver to request an SM PS transition in managed mode. This is useful when the driver has more information than the stack about possible interference, for example by bluetooth. \mbox{\Hypertarget{mac80211_8h_a189b1b1e007849ca5eb80c69d9595a5b}\label{mac80211_8h_a189b1b1e007849ca5eb80c69d9595a5b}} 
\index{mac80211.h@{mac80211.h}!ieee80211\_reserve\_tid@{ieee80211\_reserve\_tid}}
\index{ieee80211\_reserve\_tid@{ieee80211\_reserve\_tid}!mac80211.h@{mac80211.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{ieee80211\_reserve\_tid()}{ieee80211\_reserve\_tid()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \mbox{\hyperlink{dcache_8h_ad65b480f8c8270356b45a9890f6499ae}{int}} ieee80211\+\_\+reserve\+\_\+tid (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__sta}{ieee80211\+\_\+sta}} $\ast$}]{sta,  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{build_8c_aed742c436da53c1080638ce6ef7d13de}{u8}}}]{tid }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

ieee80211\+\_\+reserve\+\_\+tid -\/ request to reserve a specific T\+ID

There is sometimes a need (such as in T\+D\+LS) for blocking the driver from using a specific T\+ID so that the FW can use it for certain operations such as sending P\+TI requests. To make sure that the driver doesn\textquotesingle{}t use that T\+ID, this function must be called as it flushes out packets on this T\+ID and marks it as blocked, so that any transmit for the station on this T\+ID will be redirected to the alternative T\+ID in the same AC.

Note that this function blocks and may call back into the driver, so it should be called without driver locks held. Also note this function should only be called from the driver\textquotesingle{}s @sta\+\_\+state callback.

@sta\+: the station to reserve the T\+ID for @tid\+: the T\+ID to reserve

Returns\+: 0 on success, else on failure \mbox{\Hypertarget{mac80211_8h_a9b95b699cd0923a1f68895ccfbf368ce}\label{mac80211_8h_a9b95b699cd0923a1f68895ccfbf368ce}} 
\index{mac80211.h@{mac80211.h}!ieee80211\_restart\_hw@{ieee80211\_restart\_hw}}
\index{ieee80211\_restart\_hw@{ieee80211\_restart\_hw}!mac80211.h@{mac80211.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{ieee80211\_restart\_hw()}{ieee80211\_restart\_hw()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily void ieee80211\+\_\+restart\+\_\+hw (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__hw}{ieee80211\+\_\+hw}} $\ast$}]{hw }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

ieee80211\+\_\+restart\+\_\+hw -\/ restart hardware completely

Call this function when the hardware was restarted for some reason (hardware error, ...) and the driver is unable to restore its state by itself. mac80211 assumes that at this point the driver/hardware is completely uninitialised and stopped, it starts the process by calling the -\/$>$\mbox{\hyperlink{ceph__fs_8h_a94f8d852c02921f3079f6987b4a5d7d0}{start()}} operation. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} driver will need to reset all internal state that it has prior to calling this function.

@hw\+: the hardware to restart \mbox{\Hypertarget{mac80211_8h_a5d8c97cd445ae43d69e6dcf2feb28e86}\label{mac80211_8h_a5d8c97cd445ae43d69e6dcf2feb28e86}} 
\index{mac80211.h@{mac80211.h}!ieee80211\_resume\_disconnect@{ieee80211\_resume\_disconnect}}
\index{ieee80211\_resume\_disconnect@{ieee80211\_resume\_disconnect}!mac80211.h@{mac80211.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{ieee80211\_resume\_disconnect()}{ieee80211\_resume\_disconnect()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily void ieee80211\+\_\+resume\+\_\+disconnect (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__vif}{ieee80211\+\_\+vif}} $\ast$}]{vif }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

ieee80211\+\_\+resume\+\_\+disconnect -\/ disconnect from AP after resume

@vif\+: \&struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__vif}{ieee80211\+\_\+vif}} pointer from the add\+\_\+interface callback.

Instructs mac80211 to disconnect from the AP after resume. Drivers can use this after Wo\+W\+L\+AN if they know that the connection cannot be kept up, for example because keys were used while the device was asleep but the replay counters or similar cannot be retrieved from the device during resume.

Note that due to implementation issues, if the driver uses the reconfiguration functionality during resume the interface will still be added as associated first during resume and then disconnect normally later.

This function can only be called from the resume callback and the driver must not be holding any of its own locks while it calls this function, or at least not any locks it needs in the key configuration paths (if it supports HW crypto). \mbox{\Hypertarget{mac80211_8h_a807cba367501cc2ba87559872180865e}\label{mac80211_8h_a807cba367501cc2ba87559872180865e}}

ieee80211\+\_\+rts\+\_\+duration -\/ Get the duration field for an R\+TS frame @hw\+: pointer obtained from \mbox{\hyperlink{mac80211_8h_a6595c4d65fb5b0288de8888048d23bee}{ieee80211\+\_\+alloc\+\_\+hw()}}. @vif\+: \&struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__vif}{ieee80211\+\_\+vif}} pointer from the add\+\_\+interface callback. @frame\+\_\+len\+: the length of the frame that is going to be protected by the R\+TS. @frame\+\_\+txctl\+: \&struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__tx__info}{ieee80211\+\_\+tx\+\_\+info}} of the frame.

ieee80211\+\_\+rts\+\_\+get -\/ R\+TS frame generation function @hw\+: pointer obtained from \mbox{\hyperlink{mac80211_8h_a6595c4d65fb5b0288de8888048d23bee}{ieee80211\+\_\+alloc\+\_\+hw()}}. @vif\+: \&struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__vif}{ieee80211\+\_\+vif}} pointer from the add\+\_\+interface callback. @frame\+: pointer to the frame that is going to be protected by the R\+TS. @frame\+\_\+len\+: the frame length (in octets). @frame\+\_\+txctl\+: \&struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__tx__info}{ieee80211\+\_\+tx\+\_\+info}} of the frame. @rts\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} buffer where to store the R\+TS frame.

\index{mac80211.h@{mac80211.h}!ieee80211\_rx@{ieee80211\_rx}}
\index{ieee80211\_rx@{ieee80211\_rx}!mac80211.h@{mac80211.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{ieee80211\_rx()}{ieee80211\_rx()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily static void ieee80211\+\_\+rx (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__hw}{ieee80211\+\_\+hw}} $\ast$}]{hw,  }\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structsk__buff}{sk\+\_\+buff}} $\ast$}]{skb }\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [inline]}, {\ttfamily [static]}}

ieee80211\+\_\+rx -\/ receive frame

Use this function to hand received frames to mac80211. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} receive buffer in @skb must start with an I\+E\+EE 802.\+11 header. In case of a paged @skb is used, the driver is recommended to put the ieee80211 header of the frame on the linear part of the @skb to avoid memory allocation and/or memcpy by the stack.

This function may not be called in I\+RQ context. Calls to this function for a single hardware must be synchronized against each other. Calls to this function, \mbox{\hyperlink{mac80211_8h_a5cf5f4c5d5c64b8fca620e97a465f505}{ieee80211\+\_\+rx\+\_\+ni()}} and \mbox{\hyperlink{mac80211_8h_acdf29893df0401c02e8dd40254f14a5a}{ieee80211\+\_\+rx\+\_\+irqsafe()}} may not be mixed for a single hardware. Must not run concurrently with \mbox{\hyperlink{mac80211_8h_a975dbc285456cd9eb73321ed9634f814}{ieee80211\+\_\+tx\+\_\+status()}} or \mbox{\hyperlink{mac80211_8h_aa7cb3a408941e265e5e4bf7d27afc204}{ieee80211\+\_\+tx\+\_\+status\+\_\+ni()}}.

In process context use instead \mbox{\hyperlink{mac80211_8h_a5cf5f4c5d5c64b8fca620e97a465f505}{ieee80211\+\_\+rx\+\_\+ni()}}.

Some device drivers do not offload Add\+Ba/\+Del\+Ba negotiation, but handle rx buffer reording internally, and therefore also handle the session timer.

Trigger the timeout flow, which sends a Del\+Ba.

ieee80211\+\_\+rx\+\_\+list -\/ receive frame and store processed skbs in a list

Use this function to hand received frames to mac80211. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} receive buffer in @skb must start with an I\+E\+EE 802.\+11 header. In case of a paged @skb is used, the driver is recommended to put the ieee80211 header of the frame on the linear part of the @skb to avoid memory allocation and/or memcpy by the stack.

This function may not be called in I\+RQ context. Calls to this function for a single hardware must be synchronized against each other. Calls to this function, \mbox{\hyperlink{mac80211_8h_a5cf5f4c5d5c64b8fca620e97a465f505}{ieee80211\+\_\+rx\+\_\+ni()}} and \mbox{\hyperlink{mac80211_8h_acdf29893df0401c02e8dd40254f14a5a}{ieee80211\+\_\+rx\+\_\+irqsafe()}} may not be mixed for a single hardware. Must not run concurrently with \mbox{\hyperlink{mac80211_8h_a975dbc285456cd9eb73321ed9634f814}{ieee80211\+\_\+tx\+\_\+status()}} or \mbox{\hyperlink{mac80211_8h_aa7cb3a408941e265e5e4bf7d27afc204}{ieee80211\+\_\+tx\+\_\+status\+\_\+ni()}}.

This function must be called with B\+Hs disabled and R\+CU read lock

ieee80211\+\_\+rx\+\_\+napi -\/ receive frame from N\+A\+PI context

Use this function to hand received frames to mac80211. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} receive buffer in @skb must start with an I\+E\+EE 802.\+11 header. In case of a paged @skb is used, the driver is recommended to put the ieee80211 header of the frame on the linear part of the @skb to avoid memory allocation and/or memcpy by the stack.

This function may not be called in I\+RQ context. Calls to this function for a single hardware must be synchronized against each other. Calls to this function, \mbox{\hyperlink{mac80211_8h_a5cf5f4c5d5c64b8fca620e97a465f505}{ieee80211\+\_\+rx\+\_\+ni()}} and \mbox{\hyperlink{mac80211_8h_acdf29893df0401c02e8dd40254f14a5a}{ieee80211\+\_\+rx\+\_\+irqsafe()}} may not be mixed for a single hardware. Must not run concurrently with \mbox{\hyperlink{mac80211_8h_a975dbc285456cd9eb73321ed9634f814}{ieee80211\+\_\+tx\+\_\+status()}} or \mbox{\hyperlink{mac80211_8h_aa7cb3a408941e265e5e4bf7d27afc204}{ieee80211\+\_\+tx\+\_\+status\+\_\+ni()}}.

This function must be called with B\+Hs disabled.

{\footnotesize\ttfamily void ieee80211\+\_\+send\+\_\+eosp\+\_\+nullfunc (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__sta}{ieee80211\+\_\+sta}} $\ast$}]{pubsta,  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{dcache_8h_ad65b480f8c8270356b45a9890f6499ae}{int}}}]{tid }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

ieee80211\+\_\+send\+\_\+eosp\+\_\+nullfunc -\/ ask mac80211 to send N\+DP with E\+O\+SP @pubsta\+: the station @tid\+: the tid of the N\+DP

Sometimes the device understands that it needs to close the Service Period unexpectedly. This can happen when sending frames that are filling holes in the BA window. In this case, the device can ask mac80211 to send a Nullfunc frame with E\+O\+SP set. When that happens, the driver must have called \mbox{\hyperlink{mac80211_8h_a03309f7f29296298307ff67847b302eb}{ieee80211\+\_\+sta\+\_\+set\+\_\+buffered()}} to let mac80211 know that there are no buffered frames any more, otherwise mac80211 will get the more\+\_\+data bit wrong. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} low level driver must have made sure that the frame will be sent despite the station being in power-\/save. Mac80211 won\textquotesingle{}t call allow\+\_\+buffered\+\_\+frames(). Note that calling this function, doesn\textquotesingle{}t exempt the driver from closing the E\+O\+SP properly, it will still have to call ieee80211\+\_\+sta\+\_\+eosp when the N\+DP is sent. \mbox{\Hypertarget{mac80211_8h_a22d90e7cff4d996c4db522c162e621f2}\label{mac80211_8h_a22d90e7cff4d996c4db522c162e621f2}}

\index{mac80211.h@{mac80211.h}!ieee80211\_sta\_block\_awake@{ieee80211\_sta\_block\_awake}}
\index{ieee80211\_sta\_block\_awake@{ieee80211\_sta\_block\_awake}!mac80211.h@{mac80211.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{ieee80211\_sta\_block\_awake()}{ieee80211\_sta\_block\_awake()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily void ieee80211\+\_\+sta\+\_\+block\+\_\+awake (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__hw}{ieee80211\+\_\+hw}} $\ast$}]{hw,  }\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__sta}{ieee80211\+\_\+sta}} $\ast$}]{pubsta,  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{include_2linux_2types_8h_afaa87723b8417d40fcf45b7330261ef9}{bool}}}]{block }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

ieee80211\+\_\+sta\+\_\+block\+\_\+awake -\/ block station from waking up @hw\+: the hardware @pubsta\+: the station @block\+: whether to block or unblock

Some devices require that all frames that are on the queues for a specific station that went to sleep are flushed before a poll response or frames after the station woke up can be delivered to that it. Note that such frames must be rejected by the driver as filtered, with the appropriate status flag.

This function allows implementing this mode in a race-\/free manner.

To do this, a driver must keep track of the number of frames still enqueued for a specific station. If this number is not zero when the station goes to sleep, the driver must call this function to force mac80211 to consider the station to be asleep regardless of the station\textquotesingle{}s actual state. Once the number of outstanding frames reaches zero, the driver must call this function again to unblock the station. That will cause mac80211 to be able to send ps-\/poll responses, and if the station queried in the meantime then frames will also be sent out as a result of this. Additionally, the driver will be notified that the station woke up some time after it is unblocked, regardless of whether the station actually woke up while blocked or not. \mbox{\Hypertarget{mac80211_8h_ac25b4579e1a0d93d74256d4f9c36c60b}\label{mac80211_8h_ac25b4579e1a0d93d74256d4f9c36c60b}} 
\index{mac80211.h@{mac80211.h}!ieee80211\_sta\_eosp@{ieee80211\_sta\_eosp}}
\index{ieee80211\_sta\_eosp@{ieee80211\_sta\_eosp}!mac80211.h@{mac80211.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{ieee80211\_sta\_eosp()}{ieee80211\_sta\_eosp()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily void ieee80211\+\_\+sta\+\_\+eosp (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__sta}{ieee80211\+\_\+sta}} $\ast$}]{pubsta }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

ieee80211\+\_\+sta\+\_\+eosp -\/ notify mac80211 about end of SP @pubsta\+: the station

When a device transmits frames in a way that it can\textquotesingle{}t tell mac80211 in the TX status about the E\+O\+SP, it must clear the I\+E\+E\+E80211\+\_\+\+T\+X\+\_\+\+S\+T\+A\+T\+U\+S\+\_\+\+E\+O\+SP bit and call this function instead. This applies for P\+S-\/\+Poll as well as u\+A\+P\+SD.

Note that just like with \+\_\+tx\+\_\+status() and \+\_\+rx() drivers must not mix calls to irqsafe/non-\/irqsafe versions, this function must not be mixed with those either. Use the all irqsafe, or all non-\/irqsafe, don\textquotesingle{}t mix!

NB\+: the \+\_\+irqsafe version of this function doesn\textquotesingle{}t exist, no driver needs it right now. Don\textquotesingle{}t call this function if you\textquotesingle{}d need the \+\_\+irqsafe version, look at the git history and restore the \+\_\+irqsafe version! \mbox{\Hypertarget{mac80211_8h_a4b660b613e7c98267ee82779179cf60a}\label{mac80211_8h_a4b660b613e7c98267ee82779179cf60a}}

ieee80211\+\_\+sta\+\_\+ps\+\_\+transition -\/ PS transition for connected sta

When operating in AP mode with the I\+E\+E\+E80211\+\_\+\+H\+W\+\_\+\+A\+P\+\_\+\+L\+I\+N\+K\+\_\+\+PS flag set, use this function to inform mac80211 about a connected station entering/leaving PS mode.

This function may not be called in I\+RQ context or with softirqs enabled.

Calls to this function for a single hardware must be synchronized against each other.

@sta\+: currently connected sta @start\+: start or stop PS

When operating in AP mode with the I\+E\+E\+E80211\+\_\+\+H\+W\+\_\+\+A\+P\+\_\+\+L\+I\+N\+K\+\_\+\+PS flag set, use this function to inform mac80211 that a P\+S-\/\+Poll frame from a connected station was received. This must be used in conjunction with \mbox{\hyperlink{mac80211_8h_a4b660b613e7c98267ee82779179cf60a}{ieee80211\+\_\+sta\+\_\+ps\+\_\+transition()}} and possibly \mbox{\hyperlink{mac80211_8h_a86d3a4491a5ced96bdab330e6597fa8f}{ieee80211\+\_\+sta\+\_\+uapsd\+\_\+trigger()}}; calls to all three must be serialized. \mbox{\Hypertarget{mac80211_8h_a1e87c6c55ff8e97adbab2c3282ece225}\label{mac80211_8h_a1e87c6c55ff8e97adbab2c3282ece225}}

ieee80211\+\_\+sta\+\_\+register\+\_\+airtime -\/ register airtime usage for a sta/tid

Register airtime usage for a given sta on a given tid. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} driver must call this function to notify mac80211 that a station used a certain amount of airtime. This information will be used by the T\+XQ scheduler to schedule stations in a way that ensures airtime fairness.

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} reported airtime should as a minimum include all time that is spent transmitting to the remote station, including overhead and padding, but not including time spent waiting for a T\+X\+OP. If the time is not reported by the hardware it can in some cases be calculated from the rate and known frame composition. When possible, the time should include any failed transmission attempts.

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} driver can either call this function synchronously for every packet or aggregate, or asynchronously as airtime usage information becomes available. TX and RX airtime can be reported together, or separately by setting one of them to 0.

@pubsta\+: the station @tid\+: the T\+ID to register airtime for @tx\+\_\+airtime\+: airtime used during TX (in usec) @rx\+\_\+airtime\+: airtime used during RX (in usec) \mbox{\Hypertarget{mac80211_8h_a03309f7f29296298307ff67847b302eb}\label{mac80211_8h_a03309f7f29296298307ff67847b302eb}} 
\index{mac80211.h@{mac80211.h}!ieee80211\_sta\_set\_buffered@{ieee80211\_sta\_set\_buffered}}
\index{ieee80211\_sta\_set\_buffered@{ieee80211\_sta\_set\_buffered}!mac80211.h@{mac80211.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{ieee80211\_sta\_set\_buffered()}{ieee80211\_sta\_set\_buffered()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily void ieee80211\+\_\+sta\+\_\+set\+\_\+buffered (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__sta}{ieee80211\+\_\+sta}} $\ast$}]{sta,  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{build_8c_aed742c436da53c1080638ce6ef7d13de}{u8}}}]{tid,  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{include_2linux_2types_8h_afaa87723b8417d40fcf45b7330261ef9}{bool}}}]{buffered }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

ieee80211\+\_\+sta\+\_\+set\+\_\+buffered -\/ inform mac80211 about driver-\/buffered frames @sta\+: \&struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__sta}{ieee80211\+\_\+sta}} pointer for the sleeping station @tid\+: the T\+ID that has buffered frames @buffered\+: indicates whether or not frames are buffered for this T\+ID

If a driver buffers frames for a powersave station instead of passing them back to mac80211 for retransmission, the station may still need to be told that there are buffered frames via the T\+IM bit.

This function informs mac80211 whether or not there are frames that are buffered in the driver for a given T\+ID; mac80211 can then use this data to set the T\+IM bit (N\+O\+TE\+: This may call back into the driver\textquotesingle{}s set\+\_\+tim call! Beware of the locking!)

If all frames are released to the station (due to P\+S-\/poll or u\+A\+P\+SD) then the driver needs to inform mac80211 that there no longer are frames buffered. However, when the station wakes up mac80211 assumes that all buffered frames will be transmitted and clears this data, drivers need to make sure they inform mac80211 about all buffered frames on the sleep transition (sta\+\_\+notify() with S\+T\+A\+\_\+\+N\+O\+T\+I\+F\+Y\+\_\+\+S\+L\+E\+EP).

Note that technically mac80211 only needs to know this per AC, not per T\+ID, but since driver buffering will inevitably happen per T\+ID (since it is related to aggregation) it is easier to make mac80211 map the T\+ID to the AC as required instead of keeping track in all drivers that use this A\+PI. \mbox{\Hypertarget{mac80211_8h_acd8abceb2543371e76e21d60280e2a56}\label{mac80211_8h_acd8abceb2543371e76e21d60280e2a56}}

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{ieee80211\_sta\_set\_expected\_throughput()}{ieee80211\_sta\_set\_expected\_throughput()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily void ieee80211\+\_\+sta\+\_\+set\+\_\+expected\+\_\+throughput (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__sta}{ieee80211\+\_\+sta}} $\ast$}]{pubsta,  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{build_8c_a10e94b422ef0c20dcdec20d31a1f5049}{u32}}}]{thr }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

ieee80211\+\_\+sta\+\_\+set\+\_\+expected\+\_\+throughput -\/ set the expected tpt for a station

Call this function to notify mac80211 about a change in expected throughput to a station. A driver for a device that does rate control in firmware can call this function when the expected throughput estimate towards a station changes. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} information is used to tune the Co\+Del A\+QM applied to traffic going towards that station (which can otherwise be too aggressive and cause slow stations to starve).

@pubsta\+: the station to set throughput for. @thr\+: the current expected throughput in kbps. \mbox{\Hypertarget{mac80211_8h_a86d3a4491a5ced96bdab330e6597fa8f}\label{mac80211_8h_a86d3a4491a5ced96bdab330e6597fa8f}} 
\index{mac80211.h@{mac80211.h}!ieee80211\_sta\_uapsd\_trigger@{ieee80211\_sta\_uapsd\_trigger}}
\index{ieee80211\_sta\_uapsd\_trigger@{ieee80211\_sta\_uapsd\_trigger}!mac80211.h@{mac80211.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{ieee80211\_sta\_uapsd\_trigger()}{ieee80211\_sta\_uapsd\_trigger()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily void ieee80211\+\_\+sta\+\_\+uapsd\+\_\+trigger (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__sta}{ieee80211\+\_\+sta}} $\ast$}]{sta,  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{build_8c_aed742c436da53c1080638ce6ef7d13de}{u8}}}]{tid }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

ieee80211\+\_\+sta\+\_\+uapsd\+\_\+trigger -\/ (potential) U-\/\+A\+P\+SD trigger frame received @sta\+: currently connected station @tid\+: T\+ID of the received (potential) trigger frame

When operating in AP mode with the I\+E\+E\+E80211\+\_\+\+H\+W\+\_\+\+A\+P\+\_\+\+L\+I\+N\+K\+\_\+\+PS flag set, use this function to inform mac80211 that a (potential) trigger frame from a connected station was received. This must be used in conjunction with \mbox{\hyperlink{mac80211_8h_a4b660b613e7c98267ee82779179cf60a}{ieee80211\+\_\+sta\+\_\+ps\+\_\+transition()}} and possibly \mbox{\hyperlink{mac80211_8h_a70413b64bce5a2afda332196d30de02e}{ieee80211\+\_\+sta\+\_\+pspoll()}}; calls to all three must be serialized. I\+E\+E\+E80211\+\_\+\+N\+U\+M\+\_\+\+T\+I\+DS can be passed as the tid if the tid is unknown. In this case, mac80211 will not check that this tid maps to an AC that is trigger enabled and assume that the caller did the proper checks. \mbox{\Hypertarget{mac80211_8h_aa29f78538dbeee0b39b694265bb46e1c}\label{mac80211_8h_aa29f78538dbeee0b39b694265bb46e1c}}

ieee80211\+\_\+stop\+\_\+rx\+\_\+ba\+\_\+session -\/ callback to stop existing BA sessions

in order not to harm the system performance and user experience, the device may request not to allow any rx ba session and tear down existing rx ba sessions based on system constraints such as periodic BT activity that needs to limit wlan activity (eg.\+sco or a2dp)." in such cases, the intention is to limit the duration of the rx ppdu and therefore prevent the peer device to use a-\/mpdu aggregation.

@vif\+: \&struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__vif}{ieee80211\+\_\+vif}} pointer from the add\+\_\+interface callback. @ba\+\_\+rx\+\_\+bitmap\+: Bit map of open rx ba per tid @addr\+: \& to bssid mac address \mbox{\Hypertarget{mac80211_8h_a1b53ee336074974d6a17cf800c8e6fed}\label{mac80211_8h_a1b53ee336074974d6a17cf800c8e6fed}}

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{ieee80211\_tx\_dequeue()}{ieee80211\_tx\_dequeue()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structsk__buff}{sk\+\_\+buff}}$\ast$ ieee80211\+\_\+tx\+\_\+dequeue (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__hw}{ieee80211\+\_\+hw}} $\ast$}]{hw,  }\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__txq}{ieee80211\+\_\+txq}} $\ast$}]{txq }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

ieee80211\+\_\+tx\+\_\+dequeue -\/ dequeue a packet from a software tx queue

@hw\+: pointer as obtained from \mbox{\hyperlink{mac80211_8h_a6595c4d65fb5b0288de8888048d23bee}{ieee80211\+\_\+alloc\+\_\+hw()}} @txq\+: pointer obtained from station or virtual interface, or from \mbox{\hyperlink{mac80211_8h_a2e02475b8a6ada611c4dc8d4f2d3edf2}{ieee80211\+\_\+next\+\_\+txq()}}

Returns the skb if successful, N\+U\+LL if no frame was available.

Note that this must be called in an \mbox{\hyperlink{rcupdate_8h_a039341af5d714114b6c2eb8e6ed305e6}{rcu\+\_\+read\+\_\+lock()}} critical section, which can only be released after the S\+KB was handled. Some pointers in skb-\/$>$cb, e.\+g. the key pointer, are protected by R\+CU and thus the critical section must persist not just for the duration of this call but for the duration of the frame handling. However, also note that while in the wake\+\_\+tx\+\_\+queue() method, \mbox{\hyperlink{rcupdate_8h_a039341af5d714114b6c2eb8e6ed305e6}{rcu\+\_\+read\+\_\+lock()}} is already held.

softirqs must also be disabled when this function is called. In process context, use \mbox{\hyperlink{mac80211_8h_aa729ba5687ae4330308ca545ae8c2a6c}{ieee80211\+\_\+tx\+\_\+dequeue\+\_\+ni()}} instead. Here is the caller graph for this function\+:
% FIG 21
\mbox{\Hypertarget{mac80211_8h_aa729ba5687ae4330308ca545ae8c2a6c}\label{mac80211_8h_aa729ba5687ae4330308ca545ae8c2a6c}}

{\footnotesize\ttfamily static void ieee80211\+\_\+tx\+\_\+info\+\_\+clear\+\_\+status (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__tx__info}{ieee80211\+\_\+tx\+\_\+info}} $\ast$}]{info }\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [inline]}, {\ttfamily [static]}}

ieee80211\+\_\+tx\+\_\+info\+\_\+clear\+\_\+status -\/ clear TX status

@info\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} \&struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__tx__info}{ieee80211\+\_\+tx\+\_\+info}} to be cleared.

When the driver passes an skb back to mac80211, it must report a number of things in TX status. This function clears everything in the TX status but the rate control information (it does clear the count since you need to fill that in anyway).

N\+O\+TE\+: You can only use this function if you do N\+OT use info-\/$>$driver\+\_\+data! Use info-\/$>$rate\+\_\+driver\+\_\+data instead if you need only the less space that allows. \mbox{\Hypertarget{mac80211_8h_ab34f554873fa88d00903a079f23bf87a}\label{mac80211_8h_ab34f554873fa88d00903a079f23bf87a}}

{\footnotesize\ttfamily void \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__tx__status}{ieee80211\+\_\+tx\+\_\+status}} (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__hw}{ieee80211\+\_\+hw}} $\ast$}]{hw,  }\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structsk__buff}{sk\+\_\+buff}} $\ast$}]{skb }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

\mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__tx__status}{ieee80211\+\_\+tx\+\_\+status}} -\/ transmit status callback

Call this function for all transmitted frames after they have been transmitted. It is permissible to not call this function for multicast frames but this can affect statistics.

This function may not be called in I\+RQ context. Calls to this function for a single hardware must be synchronized against each other. Calls to this function, \mbox{\hyperlink{mac80211_8h_aa7cb3a408941e265e5e4bf7d27afc204}{ieee80211\+\_\+tx\+\_\+status\+\_\+ni()}} and \mbox{\hyperlink{mac80211_8h_a74c78cdee7aebc79accdd81c429b9f20}{ieee80211\+\_\+tx\+\_\+status\+\_\+irqsafe()}} may not be mixed for a single hardware. Must not run concurrently with \mbox{\hyperlink{mac80211_8h_a0d8cd103f19f8f5af8770c68638bcfde}{ieee80211\+\_\+rx()}} or \mbox{\hyperlink{mac80211_8h_a5cf5f4c5d5c64b8fca620e97a465f505}{ieee80211\+\_\+rx\+\_\+ni()}}.

@hw\+: the hardware the frame was transmitted by @skb\+: the frame that was transmitted, owned by mac80211 after this call Here is the caller graph for this function\+:

\mbox{\Hypertarget{mac80211_8h_a9cc235094502de05642378bcfdcb696f}\label{mac80211_8h_a9cc235094502de05642378bcfdcb696f}}

ieee80211\+\_\+tx\+\_\+status\+\_\+8023 -\/ transmit status callback for 802.\+3 frame format

Call this function for all transmitted data frames after their transmit completion. This callback should only be called for data frames which are using driver\textquotesingle{}s (or hardware\textquotesingle{}s) offload capability of encap/decap 802.\+11 frames.

This function may not be called in I\+RQ context. Calls to this function for a single hardware must be synchronized against each other and all calls in the same tx status family.

@hw\+: the hardware the frame was transmitted by @vif\+: the interface for which the frame was transmitted @skb\+: the frame that was transmitted, owned by mac80211 after this call \mbox{\Hypertarget{mac80211_8h_a54b9c24bd71dad6fa20aa0ecee5d8d88}\label{mac80211_8h_a54b9c24bd71dad6fa20aa0ecee5d8d88}}

ieee80211\+\_\+txq\+\_\+get\+\_\+depth -\/ get pending frame/byte count of given txq

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} values are not guaranteed to be coherent with regard to each other, i.\+e. txq state can change half-\/way of this function and the caller may end up with \char`\"{}new\char`\"{} frame\+\_\+cnt and \char`\"{}old\char`\"{} byte\+\_\+cnt or vice-\/versa.

@txq\+: pointer obtained from station or virtual interface @frame\+\_\+cnt\+: pointer to store frame count @byte\+\_\+cnt\+: pointer to store byte count \mbox{\Hypertarget{mac80211_8h_a931d630b0c48933e2c99523e3fcc69c0}\label{mac80211_8h_a931d630b0c48933e2c99523e3fcc69c0}} 
\index{mac80211.h@{mac80211.h}!ieee80211\_txq\_may\_transmit@{ieee80211\_txq\_may\_transmit}}
\index{ieee80211\_txq\_may\_transmit@{ieee80211\_txq\_may\_transmit}!mac80211.h@{mac80211.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{ieee80211\_txq\_may\_transmit()}{ieee80211\_txq\_may\_transmit()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \mbox{\hyperlink{include_2linux_2types_8h_afaa87723b8417d40fcf45b7330261ef9}{bool}} ieee80211\+\_\+txq\+\_\+may\+\_\+transmit (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__hw}{ieee80211\+\_\+hw}} $\ast$}]{hw,  }\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__txq}{ieee80211\+\_\+txq}} $\ast$}]{txq }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

ieee80211\+\_\+txq\+\_\+may\+\_\+transmit -\/ check whether T\+XQ is allowed to transmit

This function is used to check whether given txq is allowed to transmit by the airtime scheduler, and can be used by drivers to access the airtime fairness accounting without going using the scheduling order enfored by next\+\_\+txq().

Returns true if the airtime scheduler thinks the T\+XQ should be allowed to transmit, and false if it should be throttled. This function can also have the side effect of rotating the T\+XQ in the scheduler rotation, which will eventually bring the deficit to positive and allow the station to transmit again.

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} A\+PI \mbox{\hyperlink{mac80211_8h_a931d630b0c48933e2c99523e3fcc69c0}{ieee80211\+\_\+txq\+\_\+may\+\_\+transmit()}} also ensures that T\+XQ list will be aligned against driver\textquotesingle{}s own round-\/robin scheduler list. i.\+e it rotates the T\+XQ list till it makes the requested node becomes the first entry in T\+XQ list. Thus both the T\+XQ list and driver\textquotesingle{}s list are in sync. If this function returns true, the driver is expected to schedule packets for transmission, and then return the T\+XQ through \mbox{\hyperlink{mac80211_8h_a807cba367501cc2ba87559872180865e}{ieee80211\+\_\+return\+\_\+txq()}}.

@hw\+: pointer as obtained from \mbox{\hyperlink{mac80211_8h_a6595c4d65fb5b0288de8888048d23bee}{ieee80211\+\_\+alloc\+\_\+hw()}} @txq\+: pointer obtained from station or virtual interface \mbox{\Hypertarget{mac80211_8h_a271e4622836f50b89206df78f16c1948}\label{mac80211_8h_a271e4622836f50b89206df78f16c1948}}

{\footnotesize\ttfamily void ieee80211\+\_\+unreserve\+\_\+tid (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__sta}{ieee80211\+\_\+sta}} $\ast$}]{sta,  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{build_8c_aed742c436da53c1080638ce6ef7d13de}{u8}}}]{tid }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

ieee80211\+\_\+unreserve\+\_\+tid -\/ request to unreserve a specific T\+ID

Once there is no longer any need for reserving a certain T\+ID, this function should be called, and no longer will packets have their T\+ID modified for preventing use of this T\+ID in the driver.

Note that this function blocks and acquires a lock, so it should be called without driver locks held. Also note this function should only be called from the driver\textquotesingle{}s @sta\+\_\+state callback.

@sta\+: the station @tid\+: the T\+ID to unreserve \mbox{\Hypertarget{mac80211_8h_a35714d9fddb146ed880b6f1d899e3ac7}\label{mac80211_8h_a35714d9fddb146ed880b6f1d899e3ac7}}

ieee80211\+\_\+update\+\_\+mu\+\_\+groups -\/ set the V\+HT M\+U-\/\+M\+I\+MO groud data

wdev\+\_\+to\+\_\+ieee80211\+\_\+vif -\/ return a vif struct from a wdev @wdev\+: the wdev to get the vif for

This can be used by mac80211 drivers with direct cfg80211 A\+P\+Is (like the vendor commands) that get a wdev.

Note that this function may return N\+U\+LL if the given wdev isn\textquotesingle{}t associated with a vif that the driver knows about (e.\+g. monitor or A\+P\+\_\+\+V\+L\+AN interfaces.) \mbox{\Hypertarget{mac80211_8h_abf0e03b47dda7bd438cfa7b872b24241}\label{mac80211_8h_abf0e03b47dda7bd438cfa7b872b24241}}

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{wiphy\_to\_ieee80211\_hw()}{wiphy\_to\_ieee80211\_hw()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__hw}{ieee80211\+\_\+hw}}$\ast$ wiphy\+\_\+to\+\_\+ieee80211\+\_\+hw (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structwiphy}{wiphy}} $\ast$}]{wiphy }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

wiphy\+\_\+to\+\_\+ieee80211\+\_\+hw -\/ return a mac80211 driver hw struct from a wiphy

@wiphy\+: the \&struct wiphy which we want to query

mac80211 drivers can use this to get to their respective \&struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structieee80211__hw}{ieee80211\+\_\+hw}}. Drivers wishing to get to their own private structure can then access it via hw-\/$>$priv. Note that mac802111 drivers should not use \mbox{\hyperlink{cfg80211_8h_a8517f33ebe1fba8eed90d8b997ba715d}{wiphy\+\_\+priv()}} to try to get their private driver structure as this is already used internally by mac80211.

Return\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} mac80211 driver hw struct of @wiphy.
